# Bug register

The single list of every AmiWind bug and its current status. How tracking
works, the report template and the rules: [bug tracking](bugs/README.md).
Dated events are in the [journal](BUG_JOURNAL.md); full reports are in
[docs/bugs](bugs/README.md). CHIM engine bugs by part, with the build each was found
in: [CHIM Engine tracker](bugs/CHIM_TRACKER.md).

## Fix index

Generated from [`docs/bugs/bugs.json`](bugs/bugs.json): do not edit this table
by hand. Change the JSON (or use `tools/bug_register.py add` / `set`), then run
`tools/bug_register.py render`. A test fails if the two disagree.

`fixed` means a correction shipped in the named version with supporting
verification; candidates and mitigations stay `open`. `closed` means not a bug,
a duplicate or superseded (the status says which).

<!-- BEGIN GENERATED BUG REGISTER: edit docs/bugs/bugs.json, then run tools/bug_register.py render -->

916 bugs: 476 open, 394 fixed, 46 closed.

### Persisting, not resolved

Bugs reported two or more times. Most reports first. Each has its report lines on its page.

- [ENGINE-CRASH-EXIT-KEYBOARD-36](bugs/ENGINE-CRASH-EXIT-KEYBOARD-36.md): After AmiWind crashes, the AmigaDOS shell mangles typing: shift/caps latched, M and N never appear (reported 6 times, open)

- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's stair walk runs one map per worker: a single heavy room keeps one core busy for 13-19 minutes while the others sit idle (reported 5 times, fixed)
- [BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36](bugs/BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36.md): Umbrella: several builder phases run on one core while the rest of the machine idles, and some do work the build no longer needs (reported 5 times, open)
- [BUILD-PROFILE-REAPED-CHILD-DOUBLE-COUNT-42](bugs/BUILD-PROFILE-REAPED-CHILD-DOUBLE-COUNT-42.md): The build profiler's sampled timeline counts a finished child process twice (reported 5 times, fixed)

- [HUD-STATS-29](bugs/HUD-STATS-29.md): Health uses fixed 100; other bars hardcoded full (reported 5 times, open)

| [BUILD-CHIM-REGION-FRAMES-TAIL-43](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-REGION-FRAMES-TAIL-43.md) | The CHIM region-frame step runs its last frames on one worker while the other cores sit idle | medium | v0.0.43-dev3 (2026_10_18_v0.0.43-dev3_full-batch5_ef7e464) | open | - | - | Cause read 18 October 2026: prepare_areas_parallel passed the frames to ordered_map without a cost, so heavy frames late in the list started last and ran alone; the placement layouts of all 1,351 frames were made on one core (3 min 42 s) before the visibility rows started. Repair on branch v0.0.43-scheduler: region frames largest first (cost history 'chim-region-frames', else the references in their source cells), ordered_map history=/key= (largest first whenever a history exists), cost= on completed_map and governed_map, the image step's region and town frame maps by their history, layouts made when the visibility rows ask for them (overlapping the rows), and a sweep test that every pool caller gives a cost or a recorded reason. Verification: the next full build. |

| [BUILD-IMAGE-ALL-CHIM-PREFLIGHT-43](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-ALL-CHIM-PREFLIGHT-43.md) | The first build with every exterior on CHIM stops in the image preflight: with no classic world maps left, the disk layout check refuses with a message that names no file or limit | high | v0.0.43-dev3 (2026_10_18_v0.0.43-dev3_full-batch5_ef7e464) | open | - | - | Found 18 October 2026 in the dev3 build: after 3 h 31 min of conversion the image payload preflight stopped in 8.3 s: disk-layout: Invalid or oversized boot payload. Cause confirmed on the run: no classic open-world map is left on disk (every cell is a CHIM region frame, whose maps are written later in the image step), so the planned map list was empty and partition_batches refused it with the oversized-boot message. Repair: the preflight plans the CHIM region frame maps (recorded size bound) and their far terrain and sprites (exact), and every partition refusal names its unit and numbers; on the dev3 run the preflight passes (one 2.28 GB drive: boot 512 MiB, frame maps 128 MiB, CHIM world 1.5 GiB, every partition below 2 GiB and starting below 2 GiB). Open until the resumed dev3 image step passes. |

| [BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36](bugs/BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36.md) | Builder phases run silent: no start/end timing, no progress, no heartbeat; the CHIM stats step is a single-core tail | medium | v0.0.36-dev3 | open | - | - | 16 October 2026: Open: phase timer, heartbeat and timed image sub-phases shipped in v0.0.36 (92de7af); heartbeat/quiet-mode tests and parallel CHIM validation per frame not done. |
| [BUILD-STAGE-ALLOWANCE-STUCK-43](bugs/BUILD-STAGE-ALLOWANCE-STUCK-43.md) | A build stage's worker allowance stays at 1 after the other stages finish, so the CHIM stage runs on one core | high | v0.0.43-dev3 (2026_10_18_v0.0.43-dev3_full-batch5_ef7e464) | open | - | - | Cause found 18 October 2026: the scheduler's memory guard (tools/resource_plan.py SchedulerGuard) cut the CHIM stage 20 -> 1 workers in 3.5 minutes at 36-37 of 40 GiB while the stage's main process, not its workers, held the memory (each cut freed nothing), and grew it back only by planned 2,842 MiB units that never fit under 80 %; the visibility-row workers needed about 100 MiB each. Repair on branch v0.0.43-scheduler: the guard measures the stage's own processes (cuts only what the workers hold, grows back by their measured size, looks again when a stage ends), idle stages lend their workers to queued work (pool status files), a live supervisor writes one warning and one underused.jsonl line per stage under half its share for 60 s with units queued (never a failure), and build-summary.json/md are written at every stage end. Verification: the next full build. |

| [CHIM-TERRAIN-TEXTURE-BLOCKY-43](bugs/CHIM-TERRAIN-TEXTURE-BLOCKY-43.md) | Roads and paths on the ground look blocky: each texture square is drawn with hard edges instead of blending into its neighbours | low | v0.0.43-dev2 (2026_10_18_v0.0.43-dev2_full-batch3_b55a5ce) | open | - | - | Reported 18 October 2026 by the maintainer ('the road paths look like shit atm'; every path of the game): a path breaks into separate squares instead of reading as one track. Morrowind's land texture layer is 16 x 16 squares per cell and blends neighbouring squares; ours draws one texture per square with hard edges. Planned A/B, first option the maintainer's (20:04): (d) DIAGONAL SPLITS: where a square's texture meets a different texture on two adjacent sides (a corner of a stair-step edge), split that square along its diagonal into two triangles, each with one texture, so stair-steps become 45-degree edges (marching-squares over the texture grid; about one extra face per split square, no new textures). Then, if needed: (a) baked transition textures for the edge pairs that occur, (b) a dithered edge band baked into the surfaces, (c) finer squares along roads. Measured in faces, memory and frame time; OpenMW at the same pose. |

| [CHIM-VIVEC-DOCK-WALKWAY-43](bugs/CHIM-VIVEC-DOCK-WALKWAY-43.md) | The player cannot climb the walkway at the Vivec Hlaalu canton dock and gets stuck | medium | v0.0.43-dev2 (2026_10_18_v0.0.43-dev2_full-batch3_b55a5ce) | open | - | - | Cause found 18 October 2026: both spots (2,-11 ref 278930 and 4,-11 ref 278913) are ex_v_dock_01. The NIF's RootCollisionNode follows the planks (0 to 3.7 units), but the converter's convex pieces do not: mesh_geometry.collision_parts builds each piece's hull from its extreme points along 14 fixed directions only (a reduction since v0.0.16); on a deck that runs level and then slopes, that hull cuts across the bend and its top sits up to 19.4 units below the planks (9 % of the deck over a step under; 17 units at the reported spot, the knee-deep sinking). The closed-space split only checked surface inside the hull, so surface left outside was never seen. Fix on v0.0.43-dockwalk: a piece whose reduced hull leaves a source point more than 0.5 units outside keeps all its points (CHIM models, default on; AMIWIND_COLLISION_HULL_POINTS=extremes keeps the old method); the dock goes from 17 to 22 pieces with nothing below the planks. Island-wide sweep and gate running. |

| [ENGINE-HEAP-FIXED-SIZE-43](bugs/ENGINE-HEAP-FIXED-SIZE-43.md) | The game heap is a fixed 11 MiB from the build: with 128 MB of RAM Vivec still runs under the 2 MiB safety gap and loads pause | medium | v0.0.43-dev2 (2026_10_18_v0.0.43-dev2_full-batch3_b55a5ce) | open | - | - | Reported 18 October 2026: the maintainer plays with 128 MB, yet the heap total is 11,534,336 B (AMIWIND_HEAP_MB 11, the build's size); entering Vivec warns 'hunk-gap safety below 2 MiB', and a 'Loading...' pause showed while travelling. - [BUILD-CHIM-REGION-FRAMES-TAIL-43](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-REGION-FRAMES-TAIL-43.md): The CHIM region-frame step runs its last frames on one worker while the other cores sit idle (open)

- [BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-IDLE-START-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-IDLE-START-36.md): The chim stage of the 270-frame coast build spends its first 35-45 minutes on 1-4 cores while it holds 12-24 workers (open)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-SERIAL-TAIL-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-SERIAL-TAIL-36.md): After the parallel world build the chim stage ran its validation, heap gate and zone walk on one core (about 10 minutes of the 270-frame coast build) (open)
- [BUILD-CHIM-VIS-ROWS-SERIAL-42](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-VIS-ROWS-SERIAL-42.md): The CHIM visibility rows of a build with many new frames run on one worker at a time and print nothing for half an hour or more (open)
- [BUILD-CHIM-WORLD-SERIAL-TAIL-41](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-WORLD-SERIAL-TAIL-41.md): After the region frames the CHIM world conversion prepared the towns one after another and worked out 723 frames' visibility rows in turn: about 7 minutes on 1 to 5 of 24 cores (open)
- [BUILD-ENGINE-KEY-SDK-33](bugs/BUILD-ENGINE-KEY-SDK-33.md): The engine stage is never reused: the Amiga SDK is not part of its key (open)
- [BUILD-ENGINE-SOURCE-KEY-BROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-ENGINE-SOURCE-KEY-BROAD-36.md): Any engine source edit changes the keys of the chim and world-flora stages (open)
- [BUILD-IDLE-STAGES-33](bugs/BUILD-IDLE-STAGES-33.md): Scene-chain stages leave most of the workers they hold idle (open)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-FRAME-MAP-HEAP-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-FRAME-MAP-HEAP-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's CHIM frame-map heap check runs on one core: 66 s to 4.5 min per build while 23 cores idle (open)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-NOT-INCREMENTAL-33](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-NOT-INCREMENTAL-33.md): The image step redoes every per-map pass and the whole pack on every build (open)

- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-CORES-43](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-CORES-43.md): The image step's stair walk used 45 % of its cores in the v0.0.43-dev3 build (95 % before): the perf guard failed its walk_unit section (open)

- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-TOP-BROAD-43](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-TOP-BROAD-43.md): A pass key counts a reached module's whole import-time code: a new constant in chim/models.py rekeyed all 1,295 region frame maps (open)
- [BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36](bugs/BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36.md): Umbrella: several builder phases run on one core while the rest of the machine idles, and some do work the build no longer needs (open)

- [BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36](bugs/BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36.md): Builder phases run silent: no start/end timing, no progress, no heartbeat; the CHIM stats step is a single-core tail (open)

- [BUILD-STAIR-KEY-BOOKKEEPING-36](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-KEY-BOOKKEEPING-36.md): Stair walk keys count the progress lines (stair_progress.py) and the record bookkeeping (stair_records.py): an edit to either rekeys every walk (open)

- [FOG-TOWN-HEAVY-32](bugs/FOG-TOWN-HEAVY-32.md): Heavy fog at the Vivec Arena and in Balmora in v0.0.32-dev1 (open)
- [IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36](bugs/IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36.md): The game ships on two drive images and three world partitions; the project target is the whole game on one HDF (open)
- [INTERIOR-MORAG-TONG-FPS-43](bugs/INTERIOR-MORAG-TONG-FPS-43.md): The Morag Tong Guild interior runs at a very low frame rate (open)
- [MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33](bugs/MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33.md): A reused MiniWind build took about two hours instead of minutes (open)
- [NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33](bugs/NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33.md): Every NPC appearance is baked whole: shared body parts are converted again for each actor, and equipment cannot change (open)
- PERF-READAHEAD-29: Outdoor cell/sub-cell crossing pauses and lost read-ahead (open)
- [REMOTE-CONSOLE-LOG-COST-32](bugs/REMOTE-CONSOLE-LOG-COST-32.md): With the remote console on, every console line costs about 7-18 ms in FS-UAE (open)
- [TOWN-VIS-OCCLUSION-31](bugs/TOWN-VIS-OCCLUSION-31.md): Converted buildings, rooms and rocks do not block Quake visibility (func_wall) (open)
- [BUILD-CACHE-CHIM-UNITS-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-CHIM-UNITS-33.md): The chim stage's fingerprint changes whenever the CHIM unit cache gains units, so the stage is never reused (fixed)
- [BUILD-CACHE-NO-CUTOFF-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-NO-CUTOFF-33.md): A rebuilt stage whose outputs come out unchanged still rebuilds every stage after it (fixed)
- [BUILD-CACHE-OVERBROAD-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-OVERBROAD-33.md): CHIM builds rerun the scene chain after merges that cannot change its outputs (fixed)
- [BUILD-CACHE-PER-WORKSPACE-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-PER-WORKSPACE-33.md): Per-file caches live in each build workspace, so a build on a new volume with --reuse-from converts everything again (MiniWind media 1,195 s instead of seconds) (fixed)
- [BUILD-CELL-PROGRESS-KEY-BUGS-35](bugs/BUILD-CELL-PROGRESS-KEY-BUGS-35.md): Every bug registration stopped builds at the reuse preflight: the cell-progress key held all of docs/ (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-FRAME-SWAP-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-FRAME-SWAP-LATE-36.md): The image step builds 335 legacy maps of cells already on CHIM, then removes them before packing (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-HEAP-GATE-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-HEAP-GATE-LATE-36.md): The CHIM heap gate runs after the stair walk: a ring over the zone is found 69 minutes late (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-HULL-RING-33](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-HULL-RING-33.md): Routed and compiled CHIM model hulls grew Balmora's ring past the zone (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-KEY-UNDERDECLARED-35](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-KEY-UNDERDECLARED-35.md): The CHIM stage's fingerprint left out the tracker code it ran (8 files, 2 functions): a wrong-reuse risk (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-JOBS-AT-LAUNCH-42](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-JOBS-AT-LAUNCH-42.md): In-stage pools of the CHIM step stay at the --jobs the stage was launched with: the v0.0.42 final's stair gate walked on 2 workers with an allowance of 12 (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-NO-REUSE-42](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-NO-REUSE-42.md): The v0.0.42 final walked all 1,118 frames again: the walks of the passed dev1 build carry no check mark, because its launcher never promotes them (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36.md): CHIM frames are stair-walked again on every build: about 69 minutes for 270 frames (fixed)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STATS-SERIAL-TAIL-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STATS-SERIAL-TAIL-36.md): The chim stage ended with its statistics validating the whole CHIM world a second time, on one core with no output (7 min 44 s on 273 frames) (fixed)
- [BUILD-DOOR-REFERENCE-SERIAL-33](bugs/BUILD-DOOR-REFERENCE-SERIAL-33.md): The door step reads the whole master once per destination cell (fixed)
- [BUILD-ENV-FINGERPRINT-GLOBAL-33](bugs/BUILD-ENV-FINGERPRINT-GLOBAL-33.md): The stair rule setting invalidates the reuse cache of every build stage, including stages that never read it (fixed)
- [BUILD-GALLERY-JSON-KEY-ORDER-35](bugs/BUILD-GALLERY-JSON-KEY-ORDER-35.md): NPC gallery outputs differed between two builds only in JSON key order (cache vs fresh results) (fixed)
- [BUILD-HAND-CATALOG-SERIAL-32](bugs/BUILD-HAND-CATALOG-SERIAL-32.md): The hand-catalog stage bakes every race and sex one after another (fixed)
- [BUILD-HULL-ROUTE-BUDGET-33](bugs/BUILD-HULL-ROUTE-BUDGET-33.md): Routed standing hulls with copies overflow a legacy map's shared clipnode budget (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-AUDIO-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-AUDIO-SERIAL-36.md): The image step puts the audio payload in one file at a time while 22 of 24 cores sit idle (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-MAPS-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-MAPS-SERIAL-36.md): The image step writes the CHIM region cells' .chim maps one by one on half a core, silently (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step hashes 34,592 payload files on 3 of 24 cores (one file per pool task) (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step's world map heap audit estimates 170 maps on 5.3 of 24 cores (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-NO-RESUME-33](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-NO-RESUME-33.md): A late failure in the image step reruns the whole image step, and the failing check could have run in its first seconds (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's stair walk runs one map per worker: a single heavy room keeps one core busy for 13-19 minutes while the others sit idle (fixed)
- [BUILD-KEY-OVERBROAD-33](bugs/BUILD-KEY-OVERBROAD-33.md): A new bug page or release file list change rebuilds interior, census, harvest and the CHIM stages: an import search path counted as a read of every file under tools/ (fixed)
- [BUILD-MINIWIND-FINGERPRINT-ENGINE-33](bugs/BUILD-MINIWIND-FINGERPRINT-ENGINE-33.md): Every build stage's source fingerprint took in the engine sources through the MiniWind module (fixed)
- [BUILD-NESTED-POOL-ALLOWANCE-33](bugs/BUILD-NESTED-POOL-ALLOWANCE-33.md): Pool workers open their own pools sized to the whole stage's allowance (fixed)
- [BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36.md): Regression: the image step's BSP optimizer pass cache reused 13 of 2,658 maps; its key covers the whole image tool, so any image-code change redoes every map (fixed)
- [BUILD-ORDERED-WINDOW-33](bugs/BUILD-ORDERED-WINDOW-33.md): The ordered worker pool idles behind one slow item, and hands long items out last (fixed)
- [BUILD-OUTPUTS-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33](bugs/BUILD-OUTPUTS-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33.md): A stage that runs again never matches its old outputs (tool logs, timings, run paths), so every stage after it is rebuilt (fixed)
- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-OVERBROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-OVERBROAD-36.md): Image-pass and unit cache keys changed with code no pass runs: a new function in a shared module and edits to the cache's own key code rekeyed every character head preview twice (Vivec stage A try2 and try3: 275 of 275 recomputed) (fixed)
- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36.md): A pass key rule change carried no entries written by older trees: dev4 recomputed 2,372 optimizer maps and the exterior sky maps with code and outputs identical to dev3 (fixed)
- [BUILD-POOL-APPLY-READ-MISS-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-APPLY-READ-MISS-35.md): In a pool-mode build every reused stage was refused as the next reuse source: the reuse step's read of tools/storage_pool.py counted as a stage input (fixed)
- [BUILD-POOL-ARG-UNFINGERPRINTED-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-ARG-UNFINGERPRINTED-35.md): The balmora stage was never reusable once the shared storage pool grew past 512 MiB: its --npc-model-pool folder was hashed as an input (fixed)
- [BUILD-PROFILE-JOBS-START-ONLY-33](bugs/BUILD-PROFILE-JOBS-START-ONLY-33.md): The build profile compares a stage's cores with the workers it started with, not the ones it held (fixed)
- [BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36](bugs/BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36.md): No profiler, perf ledger or gate flagged idle cores, work done for maps that are removed, or growth of the image (fixed)
- [BUILD-RESUME-OUTPUTS-DIFFER-35](bugs/BUILD-RESUME-OUTPUTS-DIFFER-35.md): Every v0.0.35 resume refused the stages after a rerun scene-chain stage as 'outputs differ' without comparing them (fixed)
- [BUILD-REUSE-ATTRIBUTION-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-ATTRIBUTION-33.md): Two stages that ran at the same time were both refused reuse when one wrote files under a folder the other only reads (fixed)
- [BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36.md): Early cutoff compared file modes with their write bits: a stage reused by hard link (read-only) never matched the same bytes written fresh, so 10 stages after dev3's bsp rerun were refused (fixed)
- [BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33.md): A rerun with --reuse-from reused 1 of 33 stages: the builder's scratch folder made the first stages non-reusable (fixed)
- [BUILD-REUSE-TMP-UNDECLARED-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-TMP-UNDECLARED-33.md): A stage that runs while a reused stage is copied becomes non-reusable (fixed)
- [BUILD-SCENE-DIAGNOSTIC-COPY-35](bugs/BUILD-SCENE-DIAGNOSTIC-COPY-35.md): Scene-chain stages were never reusable: copying the previous scene (logs included) counted as reading another stage's diagnostics (fixed)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-MEMORY-STARVED-42](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-MEMORY-STARVED-42.md): The scheduler's memory guard held the v0.0.42 final's chim stage on 1 of its 6 workers for over an hour, and nothing said so before or while it happened (fixed)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-TABLE-KEY-35](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-TABLE-KEY-35.md): A new row in the build scheduler's stage table rebuilds stages that never read it: the table counted as import-time code of every stage (fixed)
- [BUILD-STAIR-WALK-SLOW-33](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-WALK-SLOW-33.md): The image step's stair walk spent most of its time in interpreted collision traces and brute-force headroom tests (1,751 s of the rc1c image step) (fixed)
- [BUILD-SURVEY-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33](bugs/BUILD-SURVEY-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33.md): The world-survey stage writes its wall time into its outputs, so a rerun never has the same outputs (fixed)
- [BUILD-UNDERUSE-GATE-36](bugs/BUILD-UNDERUSE-GATE-36.md): The profiler did not flag long underused phases: 7 min 44 s of CHIM statistics on 1 of 24 cores and 77 s of zone walk on 1 core showed nowhere (fixed)
- [CENSUS-LOAD-SLOW-32](bugs/CENSUS-LOAD-SLOW-32.md): The Census and Excise Office takes 8-10 s to load in FS-UAE; other scenes 0.2-1.5 s (fixed)
- [CHIM-ARENA-MEMORY-33](bugs/CHIM-ARENA-MEMORY-33.md): The Vivec Arena does not fit the CHIM zone: canton bodies are single models of 1.4-2.5 MB (fixed)
- [CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36](bugs/CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36.md): The CHIM heap scan multiplies 0/1 tables as int32: numpy's plain loop, 6 of 24 cores, about 60 s per check (fixed)
- [CHIM-MOLAG-MEMORY-38](bugs/CHIM-MOLAG-MEMORY-38.md): Tel Uvirith exterior cell (10,1) exceeds the CHIM active-ring memory budget (fixed)
- [CHIM-PVS-SLOW-33](bugs/CHIM-PVS-SLOW-33.md): Seyda Neen's CHIM visibility rows take 5.5 minutes and are rebuilt whenever the worker count changes (fixed)
- [CHIM-SEYDA-MEMORY-33](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-MEMORY-33.md): Seyda Neen's CHIM ring was modelled larger than Balmora's: the irregular ground's routed standing hull (fixed)
- [CHIM-STAIR-GATE-WORLD-RELOAD-36](bugs/CHIM-STAIR-GATE-WORLD-RELOAD-36.md): The CHIM stair gate's workers read the whole CHIM world again every time they switch frames (about 14.5 s per read on the 273-frame world) (fixed)
- [CHIM-UNIT-FP-SOURCE-LAYOUT-33](bugs/CHIM-UNIT-FP-SOURCE-LAYOUT-33.md): The same mesh is converted again for every source stage: CHIM mesh unit fingerprints include archive offsets and texture list positions (fixed)
- [CHIM-ZONE-RING-THRASH-33](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-RING-THRASH-33.md): Balmora's chunk ring does not fit the default 6 MiB CHIM zone, so the cache thrashes (fixed)
- [CHIMPORT-NO-SHARED-POOL-35](bugs/CHIMPORT-NO-SHARED-POOL-35.md): The CHIMporter kept its own unit store beside the shared hashed storage pool: no pool flag, every run folder a private copy (fixed)
- [INTERIOR-HULL-CHAIN-33](bugs/INTERIOR-HULL-CHAIN-33.md): The Arena Pit's main structure collides through one chain of 36,545 clipnodes; every trace in the room walks it (fixed)
- [SEYDA-READ-SLOW-31](bugs/SEYDA-READ-SLOW-31.md): dev1 Seyda crossings read slower than the ov700 test image (fixed)
- [BUILD-WINDOWS-DOCKER-SLOW-31](bugs/BUILD-WINDOWS-DOCKER-SLOW-31.md): Docker builds and disk-image steps on Windows folders are slow (closed)

### Bugs by family

Every bug from v0.0.30 on belongs to one family (bugs with a shared cause or area); the families and their shared rules are in [bug tracking](bugs/README.md#families).

#### Vivec preview frame and its joins to the world (`vivec-frame-edge`)

- [DOCS-VIVEC-FRAME-PLAN-36](bugs/DOCS-VIVEC-FRAME-PLAN-36.md): The docs contradict each other on how Vivec goes onto CHIM (one frame vs eight district frames vs the coordinate range) (open, medium)
- [TOWN-EDGE-UNBUILT-32](bugs/TOWN-EDGE-UNBUILT-32.md): Leaving a town into an unbuilt neighbour drops the player into a bare world map (open, high)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-FLOATING-NPC-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-FLOATING-NPC-32.md): A Vivec resident stands at the end of a walkway by the Telvanni canton with sky drawn below his feet (open, low)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-WATER-FALL-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-WATER-FALL-32.md): The sea ends at the Arena canton edge: sky below the horizon, and the player falls out of the area through the water (open, medium)
- [VIVEC-CANTON-SKY-HOLE-32](bugs/VIVEC-CANTON-SKY-HOLE-32.md): Sky shows through a Vivec canton wall seen from below (open, low)
- [VIVEC-DISTANT-BRIDGES-32](bugs/VIVEC-DISTANT-BRIDGES-32.md): Distant bridges between the Vivec cantons are not drawn (open, low)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-FRAME-EDGE-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-FRAME-EDGE-32.md): Neighbouring canton bodies end at the Arena frame edge, in view (closed, medium)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-HANDOFF-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-HANDOFF-32.md): The Arena frame's handoff area reaches into neighbouring cantons; its frame edge can be in view (closed, medium)

#### Collision shapes and climbing (stairs, ramps, walkways) (`stairs-collision`)

- [BUILD-ROUTED-FLORA-RESERVE-33](bugs/BUILD-ROUTED-FLORA-RESERVE-33.md): Routed standing hulls push a legacy open-world map past the flora collision reserve: the full build stops in world-flora (vf0779) (open, high)
- [CHIM-STAIR-GATE-POOL-BROKEN-42](bugs/CHIM-STAIR-GATE-POOL-BROKEN-42.md): The CHIM stair gate of a whole-Vivec MiniWind stopped the build: a walk worker was killed (open, high)
- [CHIM-VIVEC-DOCK-WALKWAY-43](bugs/CHIM-VIVEC-DOCK-WALKWAY-43.md): The player cannot climb the walkway at the Vivec Hlaalu canton dock and gets stuck (open, medium)
- [COLLISION-CONVEX-LOSS-32](bugs/COLLISION-CONVEX-LOSS-32.md): Convex collision proxies lose and invent authored surfaces (open, high)
- [COLLISION-HULL-CHAINS-33](bugs/COLLISION-HULL-CHAINS-33.md): 183 brush models across the island collide through standing-hull chains 512 to 31,659 clipnodes deep (open, high)
- [COLLISION-NC-FLAGS-32](bugs/COLLISION-NC-FLAGS-32.md): Morrowind no-collision and editor-marker flags (NC, NCC, MRK, AvoidNode) are ignored by the mesh converters (open, medium)
- [COLLISION-RCN-SCOPE-32](bugs/COLLISION-RCN-SCOPE-32.md): Authored collision meshes (RootCollisionNode) are used by some converters only (open, medium)
- [COLLISION-ROUTE-NO-SPLIT-36](bugs/COLLISION-ROUTE-NO-SPLIT-36.md): Routed standing hulls of dense flora (kelp, kreshweed) stay one chain 277-305 clipnodes deep: the router finds no cut, so the cell stays hull-pending (open, medium)
- [COLLISION-STAIR-SLOPE-32](bugs/COLLISION-STAIR-SLOPE-32.md): Convex collision proxies make stairs unclimbable (open, medium)
- [COLLISION-TRACE-COST-33](bugs/COLLISION-TRACE-COST-33.md): Movement traces near large models walk long standing-hull chains: tens of ms per trace on a slow 68040 (emulator-relative) (open, high)
- [COLLISION-TRACE-LIMIT-ROUTED-ROOM-36](bugs/COLLISION-TRACE-LIMIT-ROUTED-ROOM-36.md): The tool collision tracer stops on the Vivec Arena Pit's routed hull (visit limit), so its start spot cannot be traced (open, low)
- [SEYDA-BLOCK-31](bugs/SEYDA-BLOCK-31.md): Invisible obstacle blocks the path on a Seyda Neen slope (open, medium)
- [STAIR-DETECT-POSITION-41](bugs/STAIR-DETECT-POSITION-41.md): Stair step detection depends on where a frame puts the placement: a few borderline steps are found in one frame's copy and not in another's (open, low)
- [STAIR-FLIGHT-CORE-SPLIT-41](bugs/STAIR-FLIGHT-CORE-SPLIT-41.md): The stair gate marks flights per 1024-unit core: a flight that crosses a core line loses its flight mark, so its failures do not stop a build (open, medium)
- [STAIRS-MOLAG-ACCESS-38](bugs/STAIRS-MOLAG-ACCESS-38.md): Tel Uvirith walkway step and door stairs block normal walking (open, high)
- [TERRAIN-TRAP-29](bugs/TERRAIN-TRAP-29.md): v0.0.29-dev1: Player reportedly stuck on rocks beside structures (open, high)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-ACTORS-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-ACTORS-32.md): Five Vivec Arena residents fail the actor placement gate (open, high)
- [WORLD-SLOPE-STUCK-38](bugs/WORLD-SLOPE-STUCK-38.md): Player reports being stuck beside Ashlands terrain (open, high)
- [BUILD-ACTOR-GATE-CHIM-FRAMES-36](bugs/BUILD-ACTOR-GATE-CHIM-FRAMES-36.md): The actor placement gate ignores CHIM frame residents: a CHIM-only build fails with no actors, and a blocked frame resident passes silently (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-FLORA-STAIR-PLATES-BUDGET-36](bugs/BUILD-FLORA-STAIR-PLATES-BUDGET-36.md): The stair-plate collision rules raised a legacy open-world map past the BSP clip-node limit: world-flora failed on vf0779 (33,172 aggregate clipnodes > 32,767) (fixed, critical)
- [BUILD-HULL-ROUTE-BUDGET-33](bugs/BUILD-HULL-ROUTE-BUDGET-33.md): Routed standing hulls with copies overflow a legacy map's shared clipnode budget (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-STAIR-FLAG-INERT-32](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-FLAG-INERT-32.md): The stair rule build option does nothing in v0.0.32 (fixed, low)
- [CHIM-HULL-STAIR-EDGE-33](bugs/CHIM-HULL-STAIR-EDGE-33.md): With the qbsp-compiled terrain hull on Balmora's regular ground, one flight of stairs fails at a chunk edge (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-SEYDA-EXPANDED-STAIRS-40](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-EXPANDED-STAIRS-40.md): Expanded Seyda CHIM frame fails stair walks at two original references (fixed, critical)
- [CHIM-VOS-TEMPLE-WALKWAY-41](bugs/CHIM-VOS-TEMPLE-WALKWAY-41.md): Vos walkway stair starts inside the Velothi temple's collision and fails the stair gate (fixed, high)
- [STAIRS-ADDAMASARTUS-32](bugs/STAIRS-ADDAMASARTUS-32.md): A low step in the Addamasartus cave is blocked (fixed, low)
- [STAIRS-BALMORA-B01-32](bugs/STAIRS-BALMORA-B01-32.md): A Balmora Hlaalu house staircase cannot be approached from below (fixed, medium)
- [STAIRS-BALMORA-WESTSOUTH-32](bugs/STAIRS-BALMORA-WESTSOUTH-32.md): A Hlaalu hall staircase in a Balmora interior has no clear foot (fixed, medium)
- [STAIRS-EBONHEART-CHIM-36](bugs/STAIRS-EBONHEART-CHIM-36.md): Three Ebonheart flights fail the CHIM stair gate in the whole-city Vivec frame (fixed, medium)
- [STAIRS-SEYDA-LIGHTHOUSE-32](bugs/STAIRS-SEYDA-LIGHTHOUSE-32.md): Seyda Neen lighthouse stairs are blocked (outside and inside) (fixed, medium)
- [STAIRS-SEYDA-WAREHOUSE-32](bugs/STAIRS-SEYDA-WAREHOUSE-32.md): A spiral stair in the Seyda Neen warehouse tower is blocked (fixed, medium)
- [STAIRS-VIVEC-ROOMS-JOINT-36](bugs/STAIRS-VIVEC-ROOMS-JOINT-36.md): Six flight steps in three Vivec rooms are blocked where one stair piece meets the next (fixed, medium)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-TP-ARRIVAL-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-TP-ARRIVAL-32.md): dbg tp vivec_arena leaves the player at the frame origin under the water (fixed, high)
- [VIVEC-TEMPLE-STAIRS-33](bugs/VIVEC-TEMPLE-STAIRS-33.md): Palace steps and High Fane quarter flights fail the stair gate on the CHIM Vivec Temple frame (fixed, medium)
- [COLLISION-SEYDA-PREVIEW-BYPASS-32](bugs/COLLISION-SEYDA-PREVIEW-BYPASS-32.md): The legacy Seyda Neen preview builds its own convex collision (closed, low)

#### Distance drawing (horizon, fog, sky, far edges) (`distance-drawing`)

- [CHIM-TERRAIN-TEXTURE-BLOCKY-43](bugs/CHIM-TERRAIN-TEXTURE-BLOCKY-43.md): Roads and paths on the ground look blocky: each texture square is drawn with hard edges instead of blending into its neighbours (open, low)
- [FOG-TOWN-HEAVY-32](bugs/FOG-TOWN-HEAVY-32.md): Heavy fog at the Vivec Arena and in Balmora in v0.0.32-dev1 (open, medium)
- [HORIZON-FLORA-SPRITES-32](bugs/HORIZON-FLORA-SPRITES-32.md): Horizon silhouetting: not yet perfect (open, medium)
- [HORIZON-HOLES-31](bugs/HORIZON-HOLES-31.md): Distant buildings break up against the sky (open, medium)
- HORIZON-POP-29: Ashlands horizon pops or breaks up while turning (open, medium)
- [LAND-HORIZON-GAPS-29](bugs/LAND-HORIZON-GAPS-29.md): Sky visible through gaps in distant resident ground (open, medium)
- [RENDER-FOG-PASS-COST-33](bugs/RENDER-FOG-PASS-COST-33.md): The per-pixel fog and day-night sky pass costs 150-210 ms per frame on a slow 68040 (open, medium)
- [SEYDA-WALL-SHAPE-31](bugs/SEYDA-WALL-SHAPE-31.md): Dark shape pokes out of a stone wall by the Seyda Neen shore (open, low)
- [SHELL-TEXTURE-VOTE-32](bugs/SHELL-TEXTURE-VOTE-32.md): Distant shells: door and grille textures win over large sealed areas (open, low)
- SKY-STARS-28: Enlarged stars cover original night artwork (open, medium)
- SKY-VISUAL-01: Sky-only day/night remap clashed with gray distance fog (open, medium)
- TREE-PILLAR-28: Sprite-tree roots extend into unintended striped pillars (open, medium)
- [TREE-VIEW-FLOAT-38](bugs/TREE-VIEW-FLOAT-38.md): Sprite tree appears detached from terrain from particular viewing directions (open, medium)
- SKY-NIGHT-COVER-29: Dense night clouds rarely reveal moons/stars (fixed, low)
- GEO-02: Seyda Neen ground ends before the world terrain handoff (closed, medium)

#### Seyda Neen recorded stage (`seyda-recorded`)

- [BUILD-SEYDA-CONVERTED-NOT-STAGED-35](bugs/BUILD-SEYDA-CONVERTED-NOT-STAGED-35.md): A default CHIM build stopped at the image step's payload preflight: the Seyda Neen region maps are converted later in that step (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-SEYDA-RECORDED-REWRITTEN-32](bugs/BUILD-SEYDA-RECORDED-REWRITTEN-32.md): Later image passes rewrite the recorded Seyda Neen maps, so the exception is not the recorded stage (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-SEYDA-REGEN-30](bugs/BUILD-SEYDA-REGEN-30.md): Public build cannot regenerate the Seyda Neen sub-cells (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-SEYDA-CULL-STABLE-32](bugs/BUILD-SEYDA-CULL-STABLE-32.md): From-scratch builds stop in Seyda Neen terrain culling (fragment not repeat-stable) (closed, critical)
- [BUILD-SEYDA-PRIVATE-STAGES-31](bugs/BUILD-SEYDA-PRIVATE-STAGES-31.md): Repository builder cannot regenerate the shipped Seyda Neen maps (closed, high)
- [HARVEST-SEYDA-HEAP-REFUSED-32](bugs/HARVEST-SEYDA-HEAP-REFUSED-32.md): Seven Seyda Neen sub-cells lose harvest to the heap check, six of which had it in v0.0.31 (closed, medium)

- [VIVEC-ROOM-PRECACHE-LIMIT-42](bugs/VIVEC-ROOM-PRECACHE-LIMIT-42.md): Telvanni Tower was listed again although its map needs 257 model precaches; the engine holds 256 (open, medium)

- [CHIM-VIVEC-FRAME-WIDE-41](bugs/CHIM-VIVEC-FRAME-WIDE-41.md): Vivec's CHIM frame owns a 7x7 block of whole cells: Ebonheart and Seyda-side land sit in the city frame, three residents unsupported (fixed, high)

#### Build speed (`build-speed`)

- [AW-LEASE-NO-GROW-36](bugs/AW-LEASE-NO-GROW-36.md): A running build's core lease did not grow back when gates ended, and its worker pools never grow (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-REGION-FRAMES-TAIL-43](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-REGION-FRAMES-TAIL-43.md): The CHIM region-frame step runs its last frames on one worker while the other cores sit idle (open, medium)
- BUILD-CHIM-SERIAL-SECTIONS-44: The CHIM stage runs five sections on one core while units wait: visibility rows starve their workers (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-SOURCE-REPEAT-41](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-SOURCE-REPEAT-41.md): The CHIM stage parsed the whole master and read the same models again for every area: 723 source stages took 24,500 CPU seconds (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-IDLE-START-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-IDLE-START-36.md): The chim stage of the 270-frame coast build spends its first 35-45 minutes on 1-4 cores while it holds 12-24 workers (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-SERIAL-TAIL-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-SERIAL-TAIL-36.md): After the parallel world build the chim stage ran its validation, heap gate and zone walk on one core (about 10 minutes of the 270-frame coast build) (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-VIS-ROWS-SERIAL-42](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-VIS-ROWS-SERIAL-42.md): The CHIM visibility rows of a build with many new frames run on one worker at a time and print nothing for half an hour or more (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-WORLD-SERIAL-TAIL-41](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-WORLD-SERIAL-TAIL-41.md): After the region frames the CHIM world conversion prepared the towns one after another and worked out 723 frames' visibility rows in turn: about 7 minutes on 1 to 5 of 24 cores (open, medium)
- [BUILD-ENGINE-SOURCE-KEY-BROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-ENGINE-SOURCE-KEY-BROAD-36.md): Any engine source edit changes the keys of the chim and world-flora stages (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IDLE-STAGES-33](bugs/BUILD-IDLE-STAGES-33.md): Scene-chain stages leave most of the workers they hold idle (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-FRAME-MAP-HEAP-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-FRAME-MAP-HEAP-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's CHIM frame-map heap check runs on one core: 66 s to 4.5 min per build while 23 cores idle (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-NOT-INCREMENTAL-33](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-NOT-INCREMENTAL-33.md): The image step redoes every per-map pass and the whole pack on every build (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-NPC-CHECKS-LATE-44](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-NPC-CHECKS-LATE-44.md): The image step's player body and guard torch bakes fail only at the end of a full build instead of before the expensive stages (open, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-REGION-MAPS-SERIAL-43](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-REGION-MAPS-SERIAL-43.md): The image step's CHIM frame-map phases run on one core: the world read six times, ground traces on whole frames (open, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-SERIAL-32](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-SERIAL-32.md): The image step runs on one core for about 23 minutes per pass (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-CORES-43](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-CORES-43.md): The image step's stair walk used 45 % of its cores in the v0.0.43-dev3 build (95 % before): the perf guard failed its walk_unit section (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-UNDERUSED-32](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-UNDERUSED-32.md): Parts of the image step still run serially or leave most CPUs idle (open, medium)
- [BUILD-INPUTCHECK-SLOW-32](bugs/BUILD-INPUTCHECK-SLOW-32.md): The game data reference check takes over 12 minutes through Docker (open, medium)
- [BUILD-PARTITION-GROWTH-UNGATED-36](bugs/BUILD-PARTITION-GROWTH-UNGATED-36.md): Nothing gates the number of world partitions or the growth of the image (open, medium)
- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-TOP-BROAD-43](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-TOP-BROAD-43.md): A pass key counts a reached module's whole import-time code: a new constant in chim/models.py rekeyed all 1,295 region frame maps (open, medium)
- [BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36](bugs/BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36.md): Umbrella: several builder phases run on one core while the rest of the machine idles, and some do work the build no longer needs (open, high)
- [BUILD-PROGRESS-ETA-BASIS-36](bugs/BUILD-PROGRESS-ETA-BASIS-36.md): The live build progress took a stage's expected time from earlier runs of any size: dev3's chim stage was expected at 154 s and ran 1 h 48 min (open, medium)
- [BUILD-RESOURCE-PLAN-CHIM-ESTIMATE-44](bugs/BUILD-RESOURCE-PLAN-CHIM-ESTIMATE-44.md): The resource plan estimated the CHIM stage at 2 workers and 6 h 13 min; it ran on 24 workers in about 75 min (open, medium)
- [BUILD-REUSE-APPLY-SERIAL-41](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-APPLY-SERIAL-41.md): A reused stage staged its files one at a time: world terrain, scenery and flora took 8 to 12 seconds each, one after another, at the start of every reuse build (open, low)
- [BUILD-REUSE-KEYS-TOO-BROAD-35](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-KEYS-TOO-BROAD-35.md): A whole build reused no stage, and the cost was not visible before the build started (open, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-EVEN-SHARE-33](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-EVEN-SHARE-33.md): The stage scheduler splits the workers evenly between running stages, so the longest stage gets no more than the shortest (MiniWind media on 1 of 4 workers for 1,195 s) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36](bugs/BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36.md): Builder phases run silent: no start/end timing, no progress, no heartbeat; the CHIM stats step is a single-core tail (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STAGE-ALLOWANCE-STUCK-43](bugs/BUILD-STAGE-ALLOWANCE-STUCK-43.md): A build stage's worker allowance stays at 1 after the other stages finish, so the CHIM stage runs on one core (open, high)
- [BUILD-STAGE-START-SHARE-33](bugs/BUILD-STAGE-START-SHARE-33.md): A pooled stage that starts while others run gets one worker as its start value (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STAIR-GATE-OOM-42](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-GATE-OOM-42.md): The CHIM stair gate runs a whole-Vivec MiniWind out of memory: its worker pool breaks (open, high)
- [BUILD-STAIR-WALK-BORDER-COPIES-41](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-WALK-BORDER-COPIES-41.md): The CHIM stair gate walks every frame's whole border, so each cell's flights are walked many times and unreachable copies can fail a build (open, high)
- [BUILD-STATS-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-STATS-SERIAL-36.md): The CHIM stats step runs serially and silently on one core (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STORAGE-DUPLICATES-33](bugs/BUILD-STORAGE-DUPLICATES-33.md): Build storage keeps full duplicate copies of images, stage outputs and packages, and nothing removes them (open, high)
- [CHIM-LEGACY-CHAIN-33](bugs/CHIM-LEGACY-CHAIN-33.md): CHIM builds still run the legacy exterior chain: frame maps read the legacy region maps, and the open world is built (open, medium)
- [CHIM-STAIRGATE-SLOW-33](bugs/CHIM-STAIRGATE-SLOW-33.md): The CHIM stair gate takes over 10 minutes on the Balmora and Vivec Arena world (open, medium)
- [MINIWIND-NO-INTERIOR-SCOPE-42](bugs/MINIWIND-NO-INTERIOR-SCOPE-42.md): A MiniWind of one room built the whole town: no scope converted only the start room (open, medium)
- [MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33](bugs/MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33.md): A reused MiniWind build took about two hours instead of minutes (open, high)
- [MINIWIND-SCENE-CHAIN-WEIGHT-42](bugs/MINIWIND-SCENE-CHAIN-WEIGHT-42.md): Every MiniWind bakes all playable races and the opening rooms, and walks its whole frame stairs (open, medium)
- [NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33](bugs/NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33.md): Every NPC appearance is baked whole: shared body parts are converted again for each actor, and equipment cannot change (open, medium)
- [AW-CONCURRENT-BUILDS-OVERSUBSCRIBE-36](bugs/AW-CONCURRENT-BUILDS-OVERSUBSCRIBE-36.md): Concurrent master-builder builds oversubscribe the machine: a gate step fails with 'cp: Cannot allocate memory' (fixed, high)
- [AW-LEASE-MEMORY-DEADLOCK-36](bugs/AW-LEASE-MEMORY-DEADLOCK-36.md): Memory leases deadlocked builds: a 44 GiB build never fit while any gate held its 6 GiB, so builds queued for hours (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-CACHE-ABSOLUTE-PATHS-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-ABSOLUTE-PATHS-33.md): Stage fingerprints contain absolute paths, so a moved workspace or checkout reuses nothing (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-CACHE-CHIM-UNITS-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-CHIM-UNITS-33.md): The chim stage's fingerprint changes whenever the CHIM unit cache gains units, so the stage is never reused (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-CACHE-CLOSURE-WIDE-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-CLOSURE-WIDE-33.md): Stage fingerprints count every module any imported module could import, so unrelated edits rebuild most stages (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-CACHE-NO-CUTOFF-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-NO-CUTOFF-33.md): A rebuilt stage whose outputs come out unchanged still rebuilds every stage after it (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-CACHE-OVERBROAD-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-OVERBROAD-33.md): CHIM builds rerun the scene chain after merges that cannot change its outputs (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-CACHE-PER-WORKSPACE-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-PER-WORKSPACE-33.md): Per-file caches live in each build workspace, so a build on a new volume with --reuse-from converts everything again (MiniWind media 1,195 s instead of seconds) (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-CACHE-TRACE-PYTHONPATH-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-TRACE-PYTHONPATH-33.md): The stage read trace refused reuse of a reused run when another checkout was on PYTHONPATH (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-CHIM-FRAME-SWAP-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-FRAME-SWAP-LATE-36.md): The image step builds 335 legacy maps of cells already on CHIM, then removes them before packing (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-JOBS-AT-LAUNCH-42](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-JOBS-AT-LAUNCH-42.md): In-stage pools of the CHIM step stay at the --jobs the stage was launched with: the v0.0.42 final's stair gate walked on 2 workers with an allowance of 12 (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-NO-REUSE-42](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-NO-REUSE-42.md): The v0.0.42 final walked all 1,118 frames again: the walks of the passed dev1 build carry no check mark, because its launcher never promotes them (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STATS-SERIAL-TAIL-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STATS-SERIAL-TAIL-36.md): The chim stage ended with its statistics validating the whole CHIM world a second time, on one core with no output (7 min 44 s on 273 frames) (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-UNIT-HULL-KEY-33](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-UNIT-HULL-KEY-33.md): CHIM unit cache ignores the standing-hull form: a --model-hull chain build reused routed-hull model units (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-DOOR-REFERENCE-SERIAL-33](bugs/BUILD-DOOR-REFERENCE-SERIAL-33.md): The door step reads the whole master once per destination cell (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-ENV-FINGERPRINT-GLOBAL-33](bugs/BUILD-ENV-FINGERPRINT-GLOBAL-33.md): The stair rule setting invalidates the reuse cache of every build stage, including stages that never read it (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-EXCLUDE-STAGE-CLOSURE-33](bugs/BUILD-EXCLUDE-STAGE-CLOSURE-33.md): The quick test build table entered every conversion stage's fingerprint through the stage scheduler (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-FLORA-FALLBACK-RETRY-35](bugs/BUILD-FLORA-FALLBACK-RETRY-35.md): The routed-hull fallback of a flora overlay could not retry: the failed try's candidate folder was looked up from the wrong place (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-HAND-CATALOG-SERIAL-32](bugs/BUILD-HAND-CATALOG-SERIAL-32.md): The hand-catalog stage bakes every race and sex one after another (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-AUDIO-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-AUDIO-SERIAL-36.md): The image step puts the audio payload in one file at a time while 22 of 24 cores sit idle (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-MAPS-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-MAPS-SERIAL-36.md): The image step writes the CHIM region cells' .chim maps one by one on half a core, silently (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step hashes 34,592 payload files on 3 of 24 cores (one file per pool task) (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step's world map heap audit estimates 170 maps on 5.3 of 24 cores (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's stair walk runs one map per worker: a single heavy room keeps one core busy for 13-19 minutes while the others sit idle (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-JOBS-RESOLVE-PER-STAGE-32](bugs/BUILD-JOBS-RESOLVE-PER-STAGE-32.md): With automatic jobs, stages of one build plan can get different worker counts (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-MINIWIND-FINGERPRINT-ENGINE-33](bugs/BUILD-MINIWIND-FINGERPRINT-ENGINE-33.md): Every build stage's source fingerprint took in the engine sources through the MiniWind module (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-MINIWIND-STAGE-CLOSURE-33](bugs/BUILD-MINIWIND-STAGE-CLOSURE-33.md): The MiniWind plan puts the CHIM builder modules into every conversion stage's fingerprint (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-NESTED-POOL-ALLOWANCE-33](bugs/BUILD-NESTED-POOL-ALLOWANCE-33.md): Pool workers open their own pools sized to the whole stage's allowance (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-NPCLOD-PATH-ARG-34](bugs/BUILD-NPCLOD-PATH-ARG-34.md): The first --npc-lod on build stopped at its area stage: a Path in the command broke build-state.json (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36.md): Regression: the image step's BSP optimizer pass cache reused 13 of 2,658 maps; its key covers the whole image tool, so any image-code change redoes every map (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-ORDERED-WINDOW-33](bugs/BUILD-ORDERED-WINDOW-33.md): The ordered worker pool idles behind one slow item, and hands long items out last (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-PRERENDERED-PRUNE-ORDER-33](bugs/BUILD-PRERENDERED-PRUNE-ORDER-33.md): Prerendered store prune picks the superseded entry by folder name when two entries share a timestamp (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-PROFILE-JOBS-START-ONLY-33](bugs/BUILD-PROFILE-JOBS-START-ONLY-33.md): The build profile compares a stage's cores with the workers it started with, not the ones it held (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-PROFILE-REAPED-CHILD-DOUBLE-COUNT-42](bugs/BUILD-PROFILE-REAPED-CHILD-DOUBLE-COUNT-42.md): The build profiler's sampled timeline counts a finished child process twice (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36](bugs/BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36.md): No profiler, perf ledger or gate flagged idle cores, work done for maps that are removed, or growth of the image (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-TMP-UNDECLARED-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-TMP-UNDECLARED-33.md): A stage that runs while a reused stage is copied becomes non-reusable (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-JOBSHARE-32](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-JOBSHARE-32.md): The stage scheduler fixes a stage's worker share when it starts (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-LOWBUDGET-33](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-LOWBUDGET-33.md): With a small --jobs budget the stage scheduler starts nothing when more branches are ready than the budget (busy loop) (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-MEMORY-STARVED-42](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-MEMORY-STARVED-42.md): The scheduler's memory guard held the v0.0.42 final's chim stage on 1 of its 6 workers for over an hour, and nothing said so before or while it happened (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-STAGE-INTERRUPT-ABSORBED-36](bugs/BUILD-STAGE-INTERRUPT-ABSORBED-36.md): The stage wrapper absorbed Ctrl-C: a stage that handled the interrupt and returned 0 counted as passed, and the build went on (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-STAIR-WALK-SLOW-33](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-WALK-SLOW-33.md): The image step's stair walk spent most of its time in interpreted collision traces and brute-force headroom tests (1,751 s of the rc1c image step) (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-UNDERUSE-GATE-36](bugs/BUILD-UNDERUSE-GATE-36.md): The profiler did not flag long underused phases: 7 min 44 s of CHIM statistics on 1 of 24 cores and 77 s of zone walk on 1 core showed nowhere (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-EMPTY-MODEL-REPORT-40](bugs/CHIM-EMPTY-MODEL-REPORT-40.md): Terrain-only CHIM validation crashes while reporting the largest scenery model (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-PVS-SLOW-33](bugs/CHIM-PVS-SLOW-33.md): Seyda Neen's CHIM visibility rows take 5.5 minutes and are rebuilt whenever the worker count changes (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-STAIR-GATE-WORLD-RELOAD-36](bugs/CHIM-STAIR-GATE-WORLD-RELOAD-36.md): The CHIM stair gate's workers read the whole CHIM world again every time they switch frames (about 14.5 s per read on the 273-frame world) (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-PROFILE-SECTIONS-MISSING-36](bugs/BUILD-PROFILE-SECTIONS-MISSING-36.md): Long builder phases printed no elapsed time and recorded no sections: the chim stage's 44-minute stair gate was visible only from cache file times (closed, medium)
- [BUILD-WINDOWS-DOCKER-SLOW-31](bugs/BUILD-WINDOWS-DOCKER-SLOW-31.md): Docker builds and disk-image steps on Windows folders are slow (closed, medium)

#### Build reuse keys and output attribution (`build-cache-reuse`)

- [BUILD-CACHE-OWNER-FAILS-STAGE-34](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-OWNER-FAILS-STAGE-34.md): A cache entry owned by another user failed a release build four minutes in, instead of being rebuilt or caught before any stage (open, high)
- [BUILD-CACHE-PROVENANCE-41](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-PROVENANCE-41.md): Some cache entries record no time or no versions: pool keys from CHIMport have empty versions and commit, world-terrain units and stage records have no creation time or version (open, medium)
- [BUILD-ENGINE-KEY-SDK-33](bugs/BUILD-ENGINE-KEY-SDK-33.md): The engine stage is never reused: the Amiga SDK is not part of its key (open, low)
- [BUILD-POOL-READONLY-SCENE-WRITE-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-READONLY-SCENE-WRITE-35.md): A pool-mode resume failed in 17 s: npcs could not rewrite its copy of seyda.bsp (copytree kept the pool's read-only mode) (open, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-PLAN-ENV-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-PLAN-ENV-36.md): The standalone reuse plan reports environment changes the real build does not have (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STAIR-KEY-BOOKKEEPING-36](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-KEY-BOOKKEEPING-36.md): Stair walk keys count the progress lines (stair_progress.py) and the record bookkeeping (stair_records.py): an edit to either rekeys every walk (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STAIR-PASS-NOT-KEPT-41](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-PASS-NOT-KEPT-41.md): A frame's passed stair walk is not reused when the build it ran in failed, and the walker key changes with converter code (open, high)
- [CHIMPORT-POOL-CROSS-MOUNT-36](bugs/CHIMPORT-POOL-CROSS-MOUNT-36.md): CHIMport run folder and the storage pool on two mounts of one volume: hard links fail across the mounts, so pooled units were copied, not linked (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CELL-PROGRESS-KEY-BUGS-35](bugs/BUILD-CELL-PROGRESS-KEY-BUGS-35.md): Every bug registration stopped builds at the reuse preflight: the cell-progress key held all of docs/ (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-KEY-UNDERDECLARED-35](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-KEY-UNDERDECLARED-35.md): The CHIM stage's fingerprint left out the tracker code it ran (8 files, 2 functions): a wrong-reuse risk (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-DIAGNOSTIC-INPUT-REFUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-DIAGNOSTIC-INPUT-REFUSED-36.md): The cell-progress stage was never reusable: it reads the CHIM stage's statistics report, which counted as a forbidden diagnostic read (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-FLORA-RECEIPT-SECONDS-36](bugs/BUILD-FLORA-RECEIPT-SECONDS-36.md): The world flora receipt held the stage's wall time, so a rerun with the same inputs never wrote the same receipt (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-GALLERY-JSON-KEY-ORDER-35](bugs/BUILD-GALLERY-JSON-KEY-ORDER-35.md): NPC gallery outputs differed between two builds only in JSON key order (cache vs fresh results) (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-NO-RESUME-33](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-NO-RESUME-33.md): A late failure in the image step reruns the whole image step, and the failing check could have run in its first seconds (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STALE-PYTHONPATH-35](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STALE-PYTHONPATH-35.md): Builder container images put an old builder copy on PYTHONPATH (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-KEY-FUNCTION-DEFAULTS-TOP-36](bugs/BUILD-KEY-FUNCTION-DEFAULTS-TOP-36.md): Adding a function to a shared module changed the key of every stage that imports it, although no stage runs the new code (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-KEY-OVERBROAD-33](bugs/BUILD-KEY-OVERBROAD-33.md): A new bug page or release file list change rebuilds interior, census, harvest and the CHIM stages: an import search path counted as a read of every file under tools/ (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-NPC-BAKE-CACHE-KEY-40](bugs/BUILD-NPC-BAKE-CACHE-KEY-40.md): New NPC shell fallback dependencies are absent from the model cache key (fixed, critical)
- [BUILD-OUTPUTS-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33](bugs/BUILD-OUTPUTS-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33.md): A stage that runs again never matches its old outputs (tool logs, timings, run paths), so every stage after it is rebuilt (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-OVERBROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-OVERBROAD-36.md): Image-pass and unit cache keys changed with code no pass runs: a new function in a shared module and edits to the cache's own key code rekeyed every character head preview twice (Vivec stage A try2 and try3: 275 of 275 recomputed) (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36.md): A pass key rule change carried no entries written by older trees: dev4 recomputed 2,372 optimizer maps and the exterior sky maps with code and outputs identical to dev3 (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-POOL-APPLY-READ-MISS-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-APPLY-READ-MISS-35.md): In a pool-mode build every reused stage was refused as the next reuse source: the reuse step's read of tools/storage_pool.py counted as a stage input (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-POOL-ARG-UNFINGERPRINTED-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-ARG-UNFINGERPRINTED-35.md): The balmora stage was never reusable once the shared storage pool grew past 512 MiB: its --npc-model-pool folder was hashed as an input (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-RESUME-HAZARD-STATIC-35](bugs/BUILD-RESUME-HAZARD-STATIC-35.md): A resume failed in 'area': the rebuild hazard counted files of a reused stage that had run again after all (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-RESUME-OUTPUTS-DIFFER-35](bugs/BUILD-RESUME-OUTPUTS-DIFFER-35.md): Every v0.0.35 resume refused the stages after a rerun scene-chain stage as 'outputs differ' without comparing them (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-ATTRIBUTION-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-ATTRIBUTION-33.md): Two stages that ran at the same time were both refused reuse when one wrote files under a folder the other only reads (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-AUDIT-WITHOUT-REASON-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-AUDIT-WITHOUT-REASON-36.md): The reuse audit and preflight called a stage rerun because its old record was short a replaced file UNEXPECTED, although the reason explains it (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-REUSE-CASCADE-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-CASCADE-36.md): A fix in CHIM-native town code made 23 of 36 stages rebuild: the interior stage reran with different outputs and 5 rebuilds were unexpected (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36.md): Early cutoff compared file modes with their write bits: a stage reused by hard link (read-only) never matched the same bytes written fresh, so 10 stages after dev3's bsp rerun were refused (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-RECORD-RETIRED-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-RECORD-RETIRED-36.md): Early cutoff and the reuse audit lost their records when an older run was retired: stages with byte-identical outputs counted as changed (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-REDUCED-RECORD-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-REDUCED-RECORD-36.md): A stage reused from a run that had itself reused it came back without the files a later stage replaced there, so that later stage failed when it ran again (fixed, critical)
- [BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33.md): A rerun with --reuse-from reused 1 of 33 stages: the builder's scratch folder made the first stages non-reusable (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-SCENE-DIAGNOSTIC-COPY-35](bugs/BUILD-SCENE-DIAGNOSTIC-COPY-35.md): Scene-chain stages were never reusable: copying the previous scene (logs included) counted as reading another stage's diagnostics (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-TABLE-KEY-35](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-TABLE-KEY-35.md): A new row in the build scheduler's stage table rebuilds stages that never read it: the table counted as import-time code of every stage (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-SEYDA-BOUNDED-RUN-PATHS-36](bugs/BUILD-SEYDA-BOUNDED-RUN-PATHS-36.md): The actor-contact stage wrote its run folder path into 67 Seyda Neen region records, so its outputs differed in every run folder (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-SURVEY-KEY-CONFIG-35](bugs/BUILD-SURVEY-KEY-CONFIG-35.md): World survey, world terrain, world UI and CHIM stage keys counted every file under config/: a path built from a loop name read as the whole folder (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-SURVEY-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33](bugs/BUILD-SURVEY-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33.md): The world-survey stage writes its wall time into its outputs, so a rerun never has the same outputs (fixed, medium)
- [CACHE-NO-PROVENANCE-36](bugs/CACHE-NO-PROVENANCE-36.md): Cache and pool entries were keyed by SHA-256 but did not record when, by which run, from which version and commit they were made, and some recorded no output hash (fixed, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-NO-SHARED-POOL-35](bugs/CHIMPORT-NO-SHARED-POOL-35.md): The CHIMporter kept its own unit store beside the shared hashed storage pool: no pool flag, every run folder a private copy (fixed, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-RETRY-AUDIT-FAILED-36](bugs/CHIMPORT-RETRY-AUDIT-FAILED-36.md): CHIMport --retry-failed never reran cells that converted but failed an audit (memory fit, doors, hull chains): the runner marks them done (fixed, medium)
- [MWAD-WALK-ORDER-35](bugs/MWAD-WALK-ORDER-35.md): Input check results depend on directory listing order (fixed, low)
