- [CHIM-LIGHT-CONTENTS-33](bugs/CHIM-LIGHT-CONTENTS-33.md): Actor lighting and water contents ignore CHIM chunks (open, medium)
- [CHIM-MESH-PROFILE-SPLIT-36](bugs/CHIM-MESH-PROFILE-SPLIT-36.md): Seyda Neen converts the same meshes with other profiles than the other CHIM areas (texture size 64 against 32, no surface collision rule), so they are stored twice (open, low)
- [CHIM-PACK-DIRS-33](bugs/CHIM-PACK-DIRS-33.md): CHIM pack directories are fully resident and would grow to about 400 KB for the island (open, medium)
- [CHIM-PREDICT-VIVEC-LOW-36](bugs/CHIM-PREDICT-VIVEC-LOW-36.md): The CHIM memory predictor under-predicts every Vivec district frame by 28-54 %: Vivec architecture decodes to 1.3-2.3 times the bytes per triangle it was fitted on (open, medium)
- [CHIM-PVS-HOLLOW-33](bugs/CHIM-PVS-HOLLOW-33.md): Chunk visibility culls almost nothing in Balmora: houses have hollow collision shells (open, medium)
- [CHIM-READ-BUDGET-33](bugs/CHIM-READ-BUDGET-33.md): CHIM per-frame read budget can be exceeded by a whole lump (open, medium)
- [CHIM-RECEIPT-COMMIT-33](bugs/CHIM-RECEIPT-COMMIT-33.md): CHIM world receipts do not record the commit the world was built from (open, low)
- [CHIM-RECIPE-APRON-SCOPE-41](bugs/CHIM-RECIPE-APRON-SCOPE-41.md): Regional aprons lose original-cell visual recipes and regress the memory gate (open, high)
- [CHIM-RED-MOUNTAIN-SOLID-SKY-43](bugs/CHIM-RED-MOUNTAIN-SOLID-SKY-43.md): On the Red Mountain summit (cell 2,9) the player ends up inside solid and only the sky is drawn, no terrain (open, medium)
- [CHIM-SEYDA-ACTOR-CONTACT-33](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-ACTOR-CONTACT-33.md): rc1 image step refuses Seyda Neen's CHIM frame map: six actors stand 0.8-3.3 units off the CHIM ground (open, critical)
- [CHIM-SEYDA-BALMORA-LOAD-HANG-43](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-BALMORA-LOAD-HANG-43.md): Travelling from Seyda Neen to Balmora sometimes hangs on the Loading screen (boot smoke, 2 of about 5 runs) (open, high)
- [CHIM-STAIR-PLATE-BEVELS-PER-PIECE-43](bugs/CHIM-STAIR-PLATE-BEVELS-PER-PIECE-43.md): CHIM model variants take only the mesh-wide exact-bevel flag: stair plates marked for exact standing bevels get the approximate axial hull (open, medium)
- [CHIM-STAIR-STEPS-SOLID-43](bugs/CHIM-STAIR-STEPS-SOLID-43.md): Three stair meshes fail the CHIM stair walk: Redoran steps start in solid (Ald-ruhn, Maar Gan), Hlaalu steps blocked (Suran), a rope bridge starts in solid (0,8) (open, high)
- [CHIM-STATICS-FALLBACK-41](bugs/CHIM-STATICS-FALLBACK-41.md): A frame map with more than 512 streamed statics crashes when its CHIM world cannot load, instead of running without its chunks (open, medium)
- [CHIM-TILT-VARIANTS-SPLIT-33](bugs/CHIM-TILT-VARIANTS-SPLIT-33.md): Tilted placements each get their own model variant: 39,103 extra model variants on the base island, breaking store-once (open, high)
- [CHIM-UNIT-CACHE-RACE-33](bugs/CHIM-UNIT-CACHE-RACE-33.md): Two processes storing the same CHIM unit at once: the second rename fails (open, medium)
- [CHIM-VIEW-FACES-33](bugs/CHIM-VIEW-FACES-33.md): Model faces dominate each Balmora view: the streamer alone does not fix frame rate (open, medium)
- [CHIM-WINDOW-MOUNT-CROSS-CELL-33](bugs/CHIM-WINDOW-MOUNT-CROSS-CELL-33.md): A one-cell CHIM frame stops when a window placed in it mounts on a facade placed in the next cell (open, medium)
- [CHIM-WORLD-AUDIT-SCALING-33](bugs/CHIM-WORLD-AUDIT-SCALING-33.md): The CHIM world audits grow much faster than the world: the heap gate took 3.6 s for 171 frames, 473 s for 331, and over an hour for 844 (open, medium)
- [CHIM-ZONE-RELEASE-FLICKER-42](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-RELEASE-FLICKER-42.md): Full CHIM zone: distant buildings pop in and out while standing still, and the retry costs frames (open, high)
- [CHIM-ZONE-UNSATISFIABLE-EVICT-35](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-UNSATISFIABLE-EVICT-35.md): The CHIM zone evicts every unlocked block for a request no free run can hold (open, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-OCEAN-LAND-41](bugs/CHIMPORT-OCEAN-LAND-41.md): 98 seabed cells (land records entirely under water) are marked open ocean: CHIMport never plans them and the ocean admission refuses them (open, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-RETRY-LAVA-STALE-SOURCE-36](bugs/CHIMPORT-RETRY-LAVA-STALE-SOURCE-36.md): CHIMport --retry-failed cannot rerun a lava cell of the island run: its stored source stage predates the lava conversion (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-HEAP-GATE-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-HEAP-GATE-LATE-36.md): The CHIM heap gate runs after the stair walk: a ring over the zone is found 69 minutes late (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36.md): CHIM frames are stair-walked again on every build: about 69 minutes for 270 frames (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-VALIDATE-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-VALIDATE-LATE-36.md): The CHIM world is validated only after the whole chim stage: a bad chunk is found 45 minutes in (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-NATIVE-TOWN-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-NATIVE-TOWN-36.md): The image step of a CHIM-native town read legacy region maps that are never built: the Bitter Coast test build failed at the end of its image step on maps/bc000.bsp (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-LEGACY-CHAIN-DEFAULT-36](bugs/BUILD-LEGACY-CHAIN-DEFAULT-36.md): Builds still prepared CHIM towns through the legacy region-map chain by default, and a retired flag (--legacy-area) was accepted with only a warning (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-ACTOR-RING-33](bugs/CHIM-ACTOR-RING-33.md): A scripted actor step could move the CHIM chunk ring to the actor (fixed, low)
- [CHIM-ACTORS-OUTSIDE-RING-33](bugs/CHIM-ACTORS-OUTSIDE-RING-33.md): Actors outside the CHIM ring have no terrain collision (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-ALIAS-STATIC-HUNK-35](bugs/CHIM-ALIAS-STATIC-HUNK-35.md): Streamed alias statics load into the cache mid-map without being counted in the map's Hunk rest (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-ARENA-MEMORY-33](bugs/CHIM-ARENA-MEMORY-33.md): The Vivec Arena does not fit the CHIM zone: canton bodies are single models of 1.4-2.5 MB (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-BALMORA-INSET-SLIVERS-43](bugs/CHIM-BALMORA-INSET-SLIVERS-43.md): Balmora's town frame hands over 96 units inside its cells: the 8 cells around the town keep classic strips, built as 15 legacy maps (11.5 MB) with every object of those cells (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-BRUSH-BAD-DATA-35](bugs/CHIM-BRUSH-BAD-DATA-35.md): A damaged or truncated CHIM model or terrain image ends the game (Sys_Error) instead of failing that load (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-COMPILED-HULL-SHORT-36](bugs/CHIM-COMPILED-HULL-SHORT-36.md): Compiled standing hulls lose pieces: terrain up to 3 units below the ground, Hlaalu houses up to 139 units (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-DEV3-MAPLOAD-CRASH-36](bugs/CHIM-DEV3-MAPLOAD-CRASH-36.md): v0.0.36-dev3 image: the emulator exits about 2 s after a map load (JIT preset); the game stops advancing on the cycle-exact preset (fixed, critical)
- [CHIM-EFRAG-UNCAPPED-35](bugs/CHIM-EFRAG-UNCAPPED-35.md): CHIM placements link efrags without a cap: a large ring can reach the efrag pool limit and end the map (Host_Error) (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-EMPTY-POINT-HULL-36](bugs/CHIM-EMPTY-POINT-HULL-36.md): A CHIM model without collision has no point-hull node: the engine reads it as solid, or refuses it on load (fixed, critical)
- [CHIM-FAR-BAND-GAP-36](bugs/CHIM-FAR-BAND-GAP-36.md): From a CHIM area's border the ground ends at the frame edge: up to 476 units with no land before the fog plane (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-FAR-TERRAIN-33](bugs/CHIM-FAR-TERRAIN-33.md): A CHIM frame has no distant ground: beyond the active ring the land is missing (empty valleys, Vivec not visible from the world) (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-FERN-NCO-SOLID-38](bugs/CHIM-FERN-NCO-SOLID-38.md): Fire ferns marked NCO become solid in CHIM mesh conversion (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-FRAME-COORD-RANGE-33](bugs/CHIM-FRAME-COORD-RANGE-33.md): One Vivec city frame does not fit Quake's coordinate range (network coordinates are 1/8-unit shorts) (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-FRAME-EDGE-EFRAG-CRASH-36](bugs/CHIM-FRAME-EDGE-EFRAG-CRASH-36.md): R_StoreEfrags: Bad entity type 270440 ends the game: a leaf link to an entity whose model is NULL (normal play in Seyda Neen; noclip past the Vivec frame edge) (fixed, critical)
- [CHIM-FRAME-GROUND-TRACE-38](bugs/CHIM-FRAME-GROUND-TRACE-38.md): CHIM image assembly exceeds collision traversal guard in cell 6,-5 (fixed, critical)
- [CHIM-GRAFT-FAIL-NO-FLOOR-35](bugs/CHIM-GRAFT-FAIL-NO-FLOOR-35.md): A CHIM map whose frame world cannot start loses its far terrain too: the player may spawn over void (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-GRAFT-REPACK-EMPTY-33](bugs/CHIM-GRAFT-REPACK-EMPTY-33.md): A frame world repack without room for the whole ring drops every chunk: the world vanishes and the player falls under Seyda Neen (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-HARVEST-REMOVED-MAPS-33](bugs/CHIM-HARVEST-REMOVED-MAPS-33.md): A pure CHIM image stops at the save fingerprint: harvest catalogues of the removed town region maps have no matching map (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36](bugs/CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36.md): The CHIM heap scan multiplies 0/1 tables as int32: numpy's plain loop, 6 of 24 cores, about 60 s per check (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-HULL-ROUTE-NO-CUT-43](bugs/CHIM-HULL-ROUTE-NO-CUT-43.md): Two flora meshes keep a standing hull that is one chain over the depth limit although routed: 14 cells fail the hull audit (hull policy pending) (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-LAVA-RECOVER-38](bugs/CHIM-LAVA-RECOVER-38.md): Debug recovery fails to find a clear spawn after lava contact (fixed, low)
- [CHIM-LAVA-TERRAIN-AUDIT-37](bugs/CHIM-LAVA-TERRAIN-AUDIT-37.md): Terrain validation omits lava beds, surfaces and contents (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-MEASURE-EMPTY-FRAME-33](bugs/CHIM-MEASURE-EMPTY-FRAME-33.md): CHIM world measurement stops on a frame without models (every empty sea cell) (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-MIPTEX-OFFSET-UNCHECKED-35](bugs/CHIM-MIPTEX-OFFSET-UNCHECKED-35.md): CHIM texture mip offsets are taken from disk unchecked: a wrong offset makes the rasterizer read past its block (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-MODEL-ARENA-RESERVE-41](bugs/CHIM-MODEL-ARENA-RESERVE-41.md): Placed CHIM models reserve renderer nodes that their point-hull loader never allocates (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-MOLAG-MEMORY-38](bugs/CHIM-MOLAG-MEMORY-38.md): Tel Uvirith exterior cell (10,1) exceeds the CHIM active-ring memory budget (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-OCEAN-CELL-RECORD-CLASSIC-43](bugs/CHIM-OCEAN-CELL-RECORD-CLASSIC-43.md): 14 open-sea cells with a CELL record and no LAND stay classic: the build keeps a legacy map for each, the tracker counts them as open ocean and no cell list selects them (fixed, low)
- [CHIM-OCEAN-CELL-RECORD-ROUTING-43](bugs/CHIM-OCEAN-CELL-RECORD-ROUTING-43.md): The dev3 full build stops 30 minutes in, in world terrain: the 14 open-sea cells with a CELL record are routed as map-less ocean cells although each has an open-world map (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-PACK-LRU-STREAM-35](bugs/CHIM-PACK-LRU-STREAM-35.md): A streamed model keeps a sector file handle the four-file cache may close: the next stream step reads a closed file (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-PAYLOAD-PARITY-33](bugs/CHIM-PAYLOAD-PARITY-33.md): The CHIM world misses the image step's edits to Balmora (harvest mushrooms, town flora) (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-REBUILD-COST-33](bugs/CHIM-REBUILD-COST-33.md): Each CHIM crossing rebuilds the whole frame world; the cost is not measured (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-REGION-DIVIDED-CELLS-36](bugs/CHIM-REGION-DIVIDED-CELLS-36.md): Most converted land cells cannot switch to CHIM inside the open world: the legacy builder split them over several maps and a region frame replaces exactly one (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-REGION-MARGIN-STORAGE-36](bugs/CHIM-REGION-MARGIN-STORAGE-36.md): One-cell CHIM region frames store about 3.5 times their cell's terrain (the terrain-only margin) (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-REGION-POCKET-TOWN-CELLS-42](bugs/CHIM-REGION-POCKET-TOWN-CELLS-42.md): The legacy-pocket rule counts cells wholly inside a town frame as legacy: CHIM region cells around a town are left out for nothing (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-REGION-SEABED-NO-HEIGHTS-42](bugs/CHIM-REGION-SEABED-NO-HEIGHTS-42.md): The full build refuses the 98 heightless seabed cells as region cells (no original LAND), without naming the cell, although CHIMport converts them (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-STATIC-PLACE-HOST-ERROR-35](bugs/CHIM-STATIC-PLACE-HOST-ERROR-35.md): Bad flora data at CHIM chunk activation ended the session with zone locks held; the next map then stopped the program (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-STITCH-SHORELINE-36](bugs/CHIM-STITCH-SHORELINE-36.md): A CHIM area in the legacy world has no shoreline refinement: steps of up to 28 units at its border (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-TERRAIN-GRAFT-33](bugs/CHIM-TERRAIN-GRAFT-33.md): CHIM chunk terrain is a brush entity, not part of the world tree, so it does not occlude (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-TERRAIN-HULL-BEVELS-33](bugs/CHIM-TERRAIN-HULL-BEVELS-33.md): A standing box rests above the ground at convex CHIM terrain edges (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-TEXTURE-SPECKS-33](bugs/CHIM-TEXTURE-SPECKS-33.md): CHIM-drawn surfaces show bright single-texel specks that legacy frames do not (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-UNIT-FP-SOURCE-LAYOUT-33](bugs/CHIM-UNIT-FP-SOURCE-LAYOUT-33.md): The same mesh is converted again for every source stage: CHIM mesh unit fingerprints include archive offsets and texture list positions (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-VALIDATOR-ORDER-33](bugs/CHIM-VALIDATOR-ORDER-33.md): The CHIM validator walk depended on Python string hashing (fixed, low)
- [CHIM-VARIANT-YAW-DUPLICATES-36](bugs/CHIM-VARIANT-YAW-DUPLICATES-36.md): One CHIM model variant is stored several times: each frame builds it at the yaw of its own first placement (fixed, low)
- [CHIM-VIEW-LEAF-NO-PVS-35](bugs/CHIM-VIEW-LEAF-NO-PVS-35.md): A view leaf without a visibility row draws everything, with no counter saying so (fixed, low)
- [CHIM-WORLD-INDEX-FILE-LIMIT-36](bugs/CHIM-WORLD-INDEX-FILE-LIMIT-36.md): The engine refuses a CHIM world index with more than 4096 file rows; the dev3 coast world has 7,236, so every CHIM frame map fails (fixed, critical)
- [CHIM-ZONE-RING-THRASH-33](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-RING-THRASH-33.md): Balmora's chunk ring does not fit the default 6 MiB CHIM zone, so the cache thrashes (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-ZONE-TMP-NOEXEC-33](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-TMP-NOEXEC-33.md): The CHIM zone walk gate cannot load its host library when /tmp is mounted noexec (fixed, high)
- [CHIMPORT-SEABED-NO-ISLAND-42](bugs/CHIMPORT-SEABED-NO-ISLAND-42.md): Seabed cells measured as empty sea lose their island in the tracker: they drop out of the published reference and the build calls them unknown (fixed, medium)
- [CHIM-TOWN-HEAP-ROUTED-HULL-36](bugs/CHIM-TOWN-HEAP-ROUTED-HULL-36.md): With the routed terrain hull for every chunk, Balmora and Seyda Neen rings no longer fit the CHIM zone (closed, high)

#### FPU and CPU behaviour (68040/68060) (`fpu-cpu`)

- [ENGINE-060-FSCC-41](bugs/ENGINE-060-FSCC-41.md): The engine has 263 fscc instructions (float comparisons as true/false), which the 68060 traps to its support library (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-060-MUL64-41](bugs/ENGINE-060-MUL64-41.md): The 68040 engine has 151 64-bit MULS.L/MULU.L instructions (divisions by constants), which the 68060 traps to its support library (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-FLOAT-SHORT-STORE-33](bugs/ENGINE-FLOAT-SHORT-STORE-33.md): 68040 engine build stored float-to-short conversions of the animation layout parser into the wrong group (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-FPSP-MISSING-31](bugs/ENGINE-FPSP-MISSING-31.md): Boot disk loads no 68040 FPU support library; emulator hides it (open, critical)
- [ENGINE-FPU-UNIMPL-31](bugs/ENGINE-FPU-UNIMPL-31.md): Every-frame math traps on a real 68040 (sin+cos become cexp) (open, high)
- [EXTENTS-FPU-RULE-31](bugs/EXTENTS-FPU-RULE-31.md): Surface extents and lightmap sizes depended on the FPU's arithmetic (open, high)
- [FPU-SUPPORT-MMULIB-SETPATCH-41](bugs/FPU-SUPPORT-MMULIB-SETPATCH-41.md): The MMULib 68060.library stops the boot with an alert on the AmiWind boot disk, which runs no SetPatch (open, high)
- [ENGINE-FPU-DATA-DECODE-33](bugs/ENGINE-FPU-DATA-DECODE-33.md): The 68040 FPU check reads a pointer table in the engine code section as fintrz instructions (fixed, medium)
- [ENGINE-FRAME-TIME-FLOAT-35](bugs/ENGINE-FRAME-TIME-FLOAT-35.md): The main loop held the seconds since start in floats: frame times quantise after hours of play (fixed, medium)
- [ENGINE-GAMMA-POW-35](bugs/ENGINE-GAMMA-POW-35.md): The gamma table called the C library pow, which executes FPU instructions a 68040 does not implement (fixed, low)
- [TEST-FPU-STRICT-JIT-31](bugs/TEST-FPU-STRICT-JIT-31.md): Emulator strict FPU mode is silently ignored while JIT is on (closed, low)

#### Emulator measurement method (`benchmark-method`)

- [BENCH-DISK-BYTES-32](bugs/BENCH-DISK-BYTES-32.md): FS-UAE moves disk bytes at about 90 MB/s, so it cannot price what a walk reads (open, low)
- [BENCH-FSUAE-FREQ-32](bugs/BENCH-FSUAE-FREQ-32.md): FS-UAE ignores uae_cpu_frequency in cycle-exact mode (open, low)
- [BENCH-FSUAE-JIT-HANG-32](bugs/BENCH-FSUAE-JIT-HANG-32.md): An FS-UAE JIT session hung with a Windows-folder hard file mounted (open, low)
- [BENCH-HOST-STORAGE-32](bugs/BENCH-HOST-STORAGE-32.md): Emulator disk timings with the hard file in a Windows folder measure the PC, not FFS (open, low)
- [BENCH-JIT-PROFILE-32](bugs/BENCH-JIT-PROFILE-32.md): Every frame-rate and load-time figure was measured with the emulator at host speed (open, low)
- [BENCH-SESSION-DRIFT-32](bugs/BENCH-SESSION-DRIFT-32.md): Emulated disk time drifts about 1.6x between FS-UAE sessions (open, low)
- [EMU-JIT-FAULT-ADDRESS-36](bugs/EMU-JIT-FAULT-ADDRESS-36.md): FS-UAE JIT reports fault addresses during the Vivec stage A boot smoke (open, low)
- [BENCH-AWBENCH-BUFFERS-32](bugs/BENCH-AWBENCH-BUFFERS-32.md): awbench reported the stale buffer count after AddBuffers (fixed, low)

#### Disk images, partitions and launchers (`disk-image-layout`)

- [BENCH-FSUAE-PLAIN-HDF-32](bugs/BENCH-FSUAE-PLAIN-HDF-32.md): FS-UAE does not mount a plain 1.5 GiB partition hard file (open, low)
- [BUILD-HDF-BUFFERS-32](bugs/BUILD-HDF-BUFFERS-32.md): Hard disk buffer and MaxTransfer defaults depend on the disk type (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-ALL-CHIM-PREFLIGHT-43](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-ALL-CHIM-PREFLIGHT-43.md): The first build with every exterior on CHIM stops in the image preflight: with no classic world maps left, the disk layout check refuses with a message that names no file or limit (open, high)
- [EMULATOR-TEMPLATES-WORLD-32](bugs/EMULATOR-TEMPLATES-WORLD-32.md): The static emulator templates list only the boot disk, not the world disk (open, medium)
- [FFS-DIRECTORY-HASH-32](bugs/FFS-DIRECTORY-HASH-32.md): Thousands of files in one directory are slow to open on a real FFS disk (open, medium)
- [IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36](bugs/IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36.md): The game ships on two drive images and three world partitions; the project target is the whole game on one HDF (open, medium)
- [WORLD-HDF-OVER-2GIB-32](bugs/WORLD-HDF-OVER-2GIB-32.md): The world hard disk image is 32,768 bytes over 2 GiB (open, medium)
- [WORLD-THIRD-PARTITION-32](bugs/WORLD-THIRD-PARTITION-32.md): The world image now needs a fourth partition (DW2); launchers were only used with three (open, medium)
- AW-20260928-17: FS-UAE 24-bit-addressing option rejected (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-LAYOUT-GATE-AFTER-WRITE-33](bugs/BUILD-LAYOUT-GATE-AFTER-WRITE-33.md): The disk-layout gate ran only after the partitions and drives were written, and not on the dry-run image (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-WORLD-PARTITION-MOUNT-33](bugs/BUILD-WORLD-PARTITION-MOUNT-33.md): Full v0.0.33-dev1 image stops at 'Please insert volume AW_WORLD3': a partition starts beyond 2 GiB (fixed, high)
- HDF-WRITER-01: Private image refresh left file-owned blocks marked free (fixed, medium)
- [AMIGA-DISK-2GIB-LIMIT-33](bugs/AMIGA-DISK-2GIB-LIMIT-33.md): Amiga disk limits: partitions below 2 GiB and starting below 2 GiB, files well under 2 GiB, drive images below 4 GiB (closed, medium)

- [BUILD-INPUTS-UNVERIFIED-32](bugs/BUILD-INPUTS-UNVERIFIED-32.md): The builder does not check user inputs (Morrowind data, Amiga libraries) against known versions (fixed, medium)
- [MWAD-DELETED-RECORD-35](bugs/MWAD-DELETED-RECORD-35.md): Readers disagree on what a deleted record is (fixed, low)

- [MWAD-STRING-DECODE-35](bugs/MWAD-STRING-DECODE-35.md): Data readers decode record strings four different ways (fixed, low)

#### Actors and NPCs (`actors-npc`)

- [ANIMKIT-ACTOR-ABI-SITES-35](bugs/ANIMKIT-ACTOR-ABI-SITES-35.md): With the animation kit on, the image step stopped late, one tool at a time, on actor models with more than the previous 8 or 21 frames (guard torch companions last) (open, high)

- [COMBAT-RUN-ROOT-DRIFT-33](bugs/COMBAT-RUN-ROOT-DRIFT-33.md): Arena fighters' run frames carry the root's forward motion: the body slides ahead and snaps back each cycle (open, low)

- [INTERIOR-CREATURES-MISSING-41](bugs/INTERIOR-CREATURES-MISSING-41.md): No creature is placed in any interior: the resident stage takes NPC records only, so the demigod Vivec is missing from his palace (open, high)
- [NPC-ANIM-IDLE-ONLY-33](bugs/NPC-ANIM-IDLE-ONLY-33.md): NPCs have only an 8-frame idle: walking, running, swimming, hit and death are not animated (open, medium)

- [NPC-DAGOTH-BODY-DECIMATION-33](bugs/NPC-DAGOTH-BODY-DECIMATION-33.md): Dagoth Ur's body is decimated; his whole model exceeds the alias ceiling (open, medium)
- [NPC-FEMALE-SKELETON-33](bugs/NPC-FEMALE-SKELETON-33.md): Female humans are posed on the male skeleton file (base_anim.nif), the original uses base_anim_female.nif (open, low)
- [NPC-FOLLOW-FLOORS-33](bugs/NPC-FOLLOW-FLOORS-33.md): The test companion cannot find stairs to another floor of an interior; it is placed beside the player after 6 seconds (open, low)
- [NPC-HEAD-DECIMATION-33](bugs/NPC-HEAD-DECIMATION-33.md): NPC heads decimated into unrecognisable faces (open, high)
- [NPC-NEAR-SKIN-TEXELS-33](bugs/NPC-NEAR-SKIN-TEXELS-33.md): Original-head models had blurrier bodies than the budget model (open, medium)
- [NPC-NO-RUN-ANIM-35](bugs/NPC-NO-RUN-ANIM-35.md): Companions and hostile NPCs walk but never run, and the walk looks wrong: release residents had only idle frames (open, high)

- [NPC-SPEAR-ATTACK-REGRESSION-39](bugs/NPC-SPEAR-ATTACK-REGRESSION-39.md): Seyda Neen guard spear attack animation is missing in first-person view (open, critical)
- [NPC-WANDER-NOT-EXECUTED-35](bugs/NPC-WANDER-NOT-EXECUTED-35.md): Residents stand still: the original's Wander AI packages (radius, duration, idles) are decoded but not executed (open, medium)
- [NPC-WEAPON-MESH-33](bugs/NPC-WEAPON-MESH-33.md): NPCs hold no weapons or shields; combat uses them unseen (open, medium)

- AW-20260929-01: Player passes through town NPCs (fixed, medium)
